(herald "Verification of TLS 1.3 rules to assist in mTLS verification"
  (try-old-strands))

(comment "CPSA 4.4.9")
(comment "All input read from E3_client_secrecy_s_no_lemma_rules.scm")
(comment "Strand count bounded at 30")
(comment "Old strands tried first")

(defprotocol mtls13 basic
  (defrole client
    (vars (s c ca name) (cr sr random32) (x rndx) (y expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text))
    (trace (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (enc
            (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
              (hash
                (hash "finished"
                  (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
                (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
                (enc
                  (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                    spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))) c
              cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca))) (invk cpk))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (cat c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
                (enc
                  (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                    spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
                (hash
                  (hash "finished"
                    (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
                  (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                  (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
                  (enc
                    (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                      s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
                c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca))) (invk cpk)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen x))
  (defrole server
    (vars (s c ca name) (cr sr random32) (y rndx) (x expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text))
    (trace (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (enc
            (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
              (hash
                (hash "finished"
                  (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
                (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
                (enc
                  (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                    spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))) c
              cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca))) (invk cpk))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (cat c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
                (enc
                  (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                    spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
                (hash
                  (hash "finished"
                    (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
                  (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                  (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
                  (enc
                    (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                      s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
                c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca))) (invk cpk)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived"))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen y)
    (absent (y x)))
  (defrule clients-servers-distinct
    (forall ((z0 z1 strd) (c name))
      (implies
        (and (p "client" "s" z1 c) (p "client" "c" z0 c))
        (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_strd
    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_mesg
    (forall ((x mesg)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (lang (random32 atom)))

(defskeleton mtls13
  (vars (cr sr random32) (request response text) (spk cpk akey)
    (s ca c name) (x rndx) (y expt))
  (defstrand client 5 (cr cr) (sr sr) (request request)
    (response response) (spk spk) (cpk cpk) (s s) (c c) (ca ca) (x x)
    (y y))
  (deflistener
    (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))
  (non-orig (invk spk))
  (uniq-orig cr)
  (uniq-gen x)
  (goals
    (forall
      ((cr sr random32) (spk akey) (s ca name) (x rndx) (y expt)
        (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "client" z 5) (p "" z-0 2) (p "client" "cr" z cr)
          (p "client" "sr" z sr) (p "client" "spk" z spk)
          (p "client" "s" z s) (p "client" "ca" z ca)
          (p "client" "x" z x) (p "client" "y" z y)
          (p "" "x" z-0
            (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))) (non (invk spk))
          (uniq-at cr z 0) (ugen x)) (false))))
  (traces
    ((send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (enc
            (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
              (hash
                (hash "finished"
                  (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
                (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
                (enc
                  (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                    spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))) c
              cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca))) (invk cpk))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (cat c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
                (enc
                  (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                    spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
                (hash
                  (hash "finished"
                    (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
                  (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                  (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
                  (enc
                    (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                      s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
                c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca))) (invk cpk)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))
    ((recv
       (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
         (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))
      (send
        (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))

  (unrealized (0 1) (1 0))
  (preskeleton)
  (maps)
  (origs (cr (0 0)))
  (ugens (x (0 0)))

(defskeleton mtls13
  (vars (cr sr random32) (request response text) (spk cpk akey)
    (s ca c name) (x y rndx))
  (defstrand client 5 (cr cr) (sr sr) (request request)
    (response response) (spk spk) (cpk cpk) (s s) (c c) (ca ca) (x x)
    (y y))
  (deflistener
    (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))
  (defstrand server 2 (cr cr) (sr sr) (spk spk) (s s) (ca ca) (y y)
    (x x))
  (precedes ((0 0) (2 0)) ((2 1) (0 1)) ((2 1) (1 0)))
  (non-orig (invk spk))
  (uniq-orig cr)
  (uniq-gen x y)
  (absent (y x))
  (goals
    (forall
      ((cr sr random32) (spk akey) (s ca name) (x rndx) (y expt)
        (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "client" z 5) (p "" z-0 2) (p "client" "cr" z cr)
          (p "client" "sr" z sr) (p "client" "spk" z spk)
          (p "client" "s" z s) (p "client" "ca" z ca)
          (p "client" "x" z x) (p "client" "y" z y)
          (p "" "x" z-0
            (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))) (non (invk spk))
          (uniq-at cr z 0) (ugen x)) (false))))
  (operation encryption-test (added-strand server 2)
    (enc
      (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
        (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)) (0 1))
  (strand-map 0 1)
  (traces
    ((send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))

              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))

            (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))

              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
              (hash
                (hash "finished"
                  (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
                (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
                (enc
                  (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                    spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))) c
              cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca))) (invk cpk))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (cat c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca)))

              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))

    (c s ca name) (y x rndx))

    (response response) (spk spk) (cpk cpk) (s s) (c c) (ca ca) (x x)
    (y y))
  (deflistener
    (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived")))
  (defstrand server 5 (cr cr) (sr sr) (request request)
    (response response) (spk spk) (cpk cpk) (s s) (c c) (ca ca) (y y)

    (cat "s ap traffic" cr sr (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived")))

    ((4 1) (2 2)))

          (p "client" "x" z x) (p "client" "y" z y)

            (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))) (non (invk spk))
          (uniq-at cr z 0) (ugen x)) (false))))

      (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
